# DEV10 likelihood-weighted composition. Raw counts retain coverage meaning; b7 stays raw.
mwp_enemy_cache_collect_regiment_effect = {
    save_scope_as = mwp_ec_regiment
    if = {
        limit = {
            maa_current_troops_count > 0
            scope:mwp_player = {
                NOT = {
                    is_target_in_variable_list = {
                        name = mwp_ec_regiments
                        target = scope:mwp_ec_regiment
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        scope:mwp_player = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = mwp_ec_regiments
                target = scope:mwp_ec_regiment
            }
        }
        save_scope_value_as = {
            name = mwp_ec_troops
            value = maa_current_troops_count
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                maa_current_troops_count > 0
                maa_size > 0
                maa_max_troops_count > 0
            }
            save_scope_value_as = {
                name = mwp_ec_chunks
                value = {
                    value = maa_current_troops_count
                    multiply = maa_size
                    divide = maa_max_troops_count
                }
            }
            mwp_enemy_cache_dispatch_regiment_effect = {
                BAND = $BAND$
            }
        }
        else = {
            mwp_d16_resolve_enemy_chunks_effect = yes
            if = {
                limit = {
                    scope:mwp_d16_base_stack > 0
                }
                scope:mwp_player = {
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_geometry_approximate
                        value = yes
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_geometry_fallback_observations
                        add = 1
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_geometry_fallback_troops
                        add = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                    }
                    if = {
                        limit = {
                            NOT = {
                                has_variable = mwp_enemy_cache_geometry_first_regiment
                            }
                        }
                        set_variable = {
                            name = mwp_enemy_cache_geometry_first_regiment
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_regiment
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            mwp_enemy_cache_dispatch_regiment_effect = {
                BAND = $BAND$
            }
        }
    }
}

                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 20
                            divide = 1000
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_toughness
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 20
                            divide = 1000
                            multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_tmp_pursuit
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 20
                            divide = 1000
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_pursuit
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 20
                            divide = 1000
                            multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_tmp_screen
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 25
                            divide = 1000
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_screen
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 25
                            divide = 1000
                            multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    is_maa_type = pesilat_warriors
                }
                scope:mwp_player = {
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_tmp_maa
                        add = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_maa
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_skirmishers
                        add = scope:mwp_ec_chunks
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_qty_skirmishers
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_chunks
                            multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_chunks_101
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_chunks
                            multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_skirmishers
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 24
                            divide = 1000
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_damage_skirmishers
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 24
                            divide = 1000
                            multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_tmp_toughness
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 10
                            divide = 1000
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_toughness
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 10
                            divide = 1000
                            multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_tmp_pursuit
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 12
                            divide = 1000
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_pursuit
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 12
                            divide = 1000
                            multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_tmp_screen
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 5
                            divide = 1000
                        }
                    }
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_screen
                        add = {
                            value = scope:mwp_ec_troops
                            multiply = 5
                            divide = 1000
                            multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            else = {
                scope:mwp_player = {
                    change_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_unknown_regiments
                        add = 1
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_coverage
                        value = no
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        fallback = {
            scope:mwp_player = {
                change_variable = {
                    name = mwp_enemy_cache_unknown_regiments
                    add = 1
                }
                set_variable = {
                    name = mwp_enemy_cache_coverage
                    value = no
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
mwp_enemy_cache_collect_actor_effect = {
    save_scope_as = mwp_ec_actor
    mwp_read_analysis_strength_effect = { OUT = mwp_ec_actor_strength }
    scope:mwp_player = {
        set_variable = {
            name = mwp_ec_actor_maa_before
            value = var:mwp_ec_tmp_maa
        }
        set_variable = {
            name = mwp_ec_actor_knights
            value = 0
        }
    }
    every_maa_regiment = {
        mwp_enemy_cache_collect_regiment_effect = {
            BAND = $BAND$
        }
    }
    every_army = {
        every_army_maa_regiment = {
            mwp_enemy_cache_collect_regiment_effect = {
                BAND = $BAND$
            }
        }
    }
    every_knight = {
        save_scope_value_as = {
            name = mwp_ec_prowess
            value = prowess
        }
        scope:mwp_player = {
            change_variable = {
                name = mwp_ec_actor_knights
                add = 1
            }
            change_variable = {
                name = mwp_ec_tmp_knight_count
                add = 1
            }
            change_variable = {
                name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_knight_count
                add = {
                    value = 1
                    multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                }
            }
            change_variable = {
                name = mwp_ec_tmp_knight_damage
                add = {
                    value = scope:mwp_ec_prowess
                    multiply = 0.05
                }
            }
            change_variable = {
                name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_knight_damage
                add = {
                    value = scope:mwp_ec_prowess
                    multiply = 0.05
                    multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                }
            }
            change_variable = {
                name = mwp_ec_tmp_toughness
                add = {
                    value = scope:mwp_ec_prowess
                    divide = 100
                }
            }
            change_variable = {
                name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_toughness
                add = {
                    value = scope:mwp_ec_prowess
                    divide = 100
                    multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                }
            }
        }
    }
    scope:mwp_player = {
        set_variable = {
            name = mwp_ec_actor_levy
            value = {
                value = scope:mwp_ec_actor_strength
                subtract = var:mwp_ec_tmp_maa
                add = var:mwp_ec_actor_maa_before
                subtract = var:mwp_ec_actor_knights
                min = 0
            }
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_ec_tmp_levy
            add = var:mwp_ec_actor_levy
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_levy
            add = {
                value = var:mwp_ec_actor_levy
                multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
            }
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_ec_tmp_toughness
            add = {
                value = var:mwp_ec_actor_levy
                divide = 100
            }
        }
        change_variable = {
            name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_toughness
            add = {
                value = var:mwp_ec_actor_levy
                divide = 100
                multiply = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
            }
        }
    }
}
mwp_enemy_cache_queue_actor_effect = {
    save_scope_as = mwp_ec_queue_actor
    scope:mwp_player = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                scope:mwp_ec_queue_actor != this
                scope:mwp_ec_queue_actor = {
                    is_alive = yes
                }
                NOT = {
                    is_target_in_variable_list = {
                        name = mwp_ec_actors
                        target = scope:mwp_ec_queue_actor
                    }
                }
            }
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = mwp_ec_actors
                target = scope:mwp_ec_queue_actor
            }
        }
    }
}
mwp_enemy_cache_prepare_scenario_effect = {
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
        value = 1
    }
    var:mwp_ec_rival = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable = mwp_war_likelihood_owner
                var:mwp_war_likelihood_owner = scope:mwp_player
                has_variable = mwp_war_likelihood_stamp
                var:mwp_war_likelihood_stamp = scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_war_scan_stamp
            }
            save_scope_value_as = {
                name = mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                value = var:mwp_war_likelihood_weight
            }
            scope:mwp_player = {
                set_variable = {
                    name = mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                    value = scope:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
                }
            }
        }
    }
    clear_variable_list = mwp_ec_actors
    clear_variable_list = mwp_ec_regiments
    add_to_variable_list = {
        name = mwp_ec_actors
        target = var:mwp_ec_rival
    }
    remove_variable = mwp_ec_actual_war
    every_character_war = {
        limit = {
            is_participant = scope:mwp_player
            is_participant = scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_ec_rival
            OR = {
                AND = {
                    is_attacker = scope:mwp_player
                    is_defender = scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_ec_rival
                }
                AND = {
                    is_defender = scope:mwp_player
                    is_attacker = scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_ec_rival
                }
            }
        }
        scope:mwp_player = {
            set_variable = {
                name = mwp_ec_actual_war
                value = yes
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                is_attacker = scope:mwp_player
            }
            every_war_defender = {
                mwp_enemy_cache_queue_actor_effect = yes
            }
        }
        else = {
            every_war_attacker = {
                mwp_enemy_cache_queue_actor_effect = yes
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = {
                has_variable = mwp_ec_actual_war
            }
        }
        var:mwp_ec_rival = {
            save_scope_as = mwp_ec_primary
            every_ally = {
                limit = {
                    is_alive = yes
                    this != scope:mwp_player
                    can_potentially_call_ally_trigger = {
                        JOINER = this
                        WARRIOR = scope:mwp_ec_primary
                    }
                }
                mwp_enemy_cache_queue_actor_effect = yes
            }
        }
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_strength
        value = 0
    }
    every_in_list = {
        variable = mwp_ec_actors
        mwp_read_analysis_strength_effect = { OUT = mwp_ec_actor_strength }
        scope:mwp_player = {
            change_variable = {
                name = mwp_ec_strength
                add = scope:mwp_ec_actor_strength
            }
        }
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_maa
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_toughness
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_pursuit
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_screen
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_siege
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_tier
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_levy
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_knight_damage
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_knight_count
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_archer_cavalry
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_archer_cavalry
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_archers
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_archers
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_camel_cavalry
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_camel_cavalry
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_elephant_cavalry
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_elephant_cavalry
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_gunpowder
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_gunpowder
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_heavy_cavalry
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_heavy_cavalry
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_heavy_infantry
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_heavy_infantry
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_light_cavalry
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_light_cavalry
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_nomadic_horde
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_nomadic_horde
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_peasant_militia
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_peasant_militia
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_pikemen
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_pikemen
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_siege_weapon
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_siege_weapon
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_qty_skirmishers
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_damage_skirmishers
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_siege_tier0
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_siege_tier1
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_siege_tier2
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_siege_tier3
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_siege_tier4
        value = 0
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_tmp_siege_tier5
        value = 0
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            var:mwp_ec_strength <= 500
        }
        mwp_enemy_cache_collect_scenario_effect = {
            BAND = 1
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            var:mwp_ec_strength <= 2000
        }
        mwp_enemy_cache_collect_scenario_effect = {
            BAND = 2
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            var:mwp_ec_strength <= 8000
        }
        mwp_enemy_cache_collect_scenario_effect = {
            BAND = 3
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            var:mwp_ec_strength <= 32000
        }
        mwp_enemy_cache_collect_scenario_effect = {
            BAND = 4
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            var:mwp_ec_strength <= 128000
        }
        mwp_enemy_cache_collect_scenario_effect = {
            BAND = 5
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            always = yes
        }
        mwp_enemy_cache_collect_scenario_effect = {
            BAND = 6
        }
    }
}
mwp_enemy_cache_collect_scenario_effect = {
    change_variable = {
        name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_weight
        add = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
    }
    change_variable = {
        name = mwp_enemy_cache_weight_sum
        add = var:mwp_ec_likelihood_weight
    }
    change_variable = {
        name = mwp_enemy_cache_b$BAND$_count
        add = 1
    }
    change_variable = {
        name = mwp_enemy_cache_rival_count
        add = 1
    }
    every_in_list = {
        variable = mwp_ec_actors
        mwp_enemy_cache_collect_actor_effect = {
            BAND = $BAND$
        }
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_raw_damage
        value = {
            value = var:mwp_ec_tmp_levy
            divide = 100
            add = var:mwp_ec_tmp_knight_damage

            add = var:mwp_ec_tmp_damage_archers

            add = var:mwp_ec_tmp_damage_gunpowder

        }

            name = mwp_enemy_cache_peak_rival
            value = var:mwp_ec_rival
        }
        set_variable = {
            name = mwp_enemy_cache_b7_maa
            value = var:mwp_ec_tmp_maa
        }
        set_variable = {
            name = mwp_enemy_cache_b7_toughness
            value = var:mwp_ec_tmp_toughness
        }
        set_variable = {
            name = mwp_enemy_cache_b7_pursuit
            value = var:mwp_ec_tmp_pursuit
        }
        set_variable = {
            name = mwp_enemy_cache_b7_screen
            value = var:mwp_ec_tmp_screen
        }
        set_variable = {

            value = var:mwp_ec_tmp_damage_peasant_militia
        }
        set_variable = {
            name = mwp_enemy_cache_b7_qty_pikemen

        clear_variable_list = mwp_ec_peak_regiments
        if = {
            limit = {
                has_variable_list = mwp_ec_regiments
            }
            every_in_list = {
                variable = mwp_ec_regiments
                save_scope_as = mwp_ec_peak_regiment
                scope:mwp_player = {

                        name = mwp_ec_peak_regiments
                        target = scope:mwp_ec_peak_regiment
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

mwp_enemy_cache_consider_fort_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_holding = yes
        }
        save_scope_as = mwp_ec_fort
        save_scope_value_as = {
            name = mwp_ec_fort_key
            value = building_max_garrison
        }
        save_scope_value_as = {
            name = mwp_ec_fort_level
            value = scope:mwp_war_fort_level
        }
        scope:mwp_player = {
            if = {
                limit = {
                    scope:mwp_ec_fort_level < 4
                }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            NOT = {
                                has_variable = mwp_ec_fort_band0
                            }
                            var:mwp_ec_fort_band0_key < scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                        }
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_band0
                        value = scope:mwp_ec_fort
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_band0_key
                        value = scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                    }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    scope:mwp_ec_fort_level < 6
                }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            NOT = {
                                has_variable = mwp_ec_fort_band1
                            }
                            var:mwp_ec_fort_band1_key < scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                        }
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_band1
                        value = scope:mwp_ec_fort
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_band1_key
                        value = scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                    }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    scope:mwp_ec_fort_level < 11
                }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            NOT = {
                                has_variable = mwp_ec_fort_band2
                            }
                            var:mwp_ec_fort_band2_key < scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                        }
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_band2
                        value = scope:mwp_ec_fort
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_band2_key
                        value = scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                    }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    scope:mwp_ec_fort_level < 16
                }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            NOT = {
                                has_variable = mwp_ec_fort_band3
                            }
                            var:mwp_ec_fort_band3_key < scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                        }
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_band3
                        value = scope:mwp_ec_fort
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_band3_key
                        value = scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                    }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    scope:mwp_ec_fort_level < 31
                }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            NOT = {
                                has_variable = mwp_ec_fort_band4
                            }
                            var:mwp_ec_fort_band4_key < scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                        }
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_band4
                        value = scope:mwp_ec_fort
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_band4_key
                        value = scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                    }
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    always = yes
                }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        OR = {
                            NOT = {
                                has_variable = mwp_ec_fort_band5
                            }
                            var:mwp_ec_fort_band5_key < scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                        }
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_band5
                        value = scope:mwp_ec_fort
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_band5_key
                        value = scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                    }
                }
            }
            if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        NOT = {
                            has_variable = mwp_ec_fort_top1
                        }
                        var:mwp_ec_fort_top1_key < scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                    }
                }
                if = {
                    limit = {
                        has_variable = mwp_ec_fort_top1
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_top2
                        value = var:mwp_ec_fort_top1
                    }
                    set_variable = {
                        name = mwp_ec_fort_top2_key
                        value = var:mwp_ec_fort_top1_key
                    }
                }
                set_variable = {
                    name = mwp_ec_fort_top1
                    value = scope:mwp_ec_fort
                }
                set_variable = {
                    name = mwp_ec_fort_top1_key
                    value = scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                }
            }
            else_if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = {
                        NOT = {
                            has_variable = mwp_ec_fort_top2
                        }
                        var:mwp_ec_fort_top2_key < scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                    }
                }
                set_variable = {
                    name = mwp_ec_fort_top2
                    value = scope:mwp_ec_fort
                }
                set_variable = {
                    name = mwp_ec_fort_top2_key
                    value = scope:mwp_ec_fort_key
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
mwp_enemy_cache_add_fort_effect = {
    save_scope_as = mwp_ec_fort
    scope:mwp_player = {
        if = {
            limit = {
                NOT = {
                    is_target_in_variable_list = {
                        name = mwp_enemy_cache_fort_samples
                        target = scope:mwp_ec_fort
                    }
                }
            }
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = mwp_enemy_cache_fort_samples
                target = scope:mwp_ec_fort
            }
            change_variable = {
                name = mwp_enemy_cache_fort_count
                add = 1
            }
        }
    }
}
